### [229] [Parason: Revealing Subtask and Trial Parallelism in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24658)
*Zhengyang Zhang,Zijian Zhang,Jiaxuan Gao,Shusheng Xu,Yi Wu,Song Han,Ligeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Parason框架，系统地揭示了大型语言模型推理中存在的两种并行形式——子任务并行（Subtask Parallelism）和试验并行（Trial Parallelism），并通过上下文无关语法将串行推理轨迹转化为结构化并行轨迹，结合并行性感知的PA-GRPO强化学习算法训练模型，在推理时通过工具调用执行并行结构，在AIME24和AIME25数学推理基准上实现约1.7倍加速且保持竞争力精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的自回归解码按顺序执行长推理轨迹，导致困难任务存在严重的延迟问题（可长达数天甚至数周）。虽然并行推理是自然的解决方案，但现有系统主要关注子任务并行，忽略了另一种普遍存在的并行形式——试验并行，即多个推测性尝试并行探索、验证和聚合竞争性假设。本文旨在揭示并利用两种形式的并行性来显著降低推理延迟。

Method: Parason方法包含三个关键步骤：(1) 通过分析识别试验并行在DeepSeek-V4的HLE推理步骤中占65.5%的可并行计算量，且随问题难度增加而更加主导；(2) 使用上下文无关文法将串行推理轨迹转换为结构化并行轨迹；(3) 采用并行性感知的组相对策略优化（PA-GRPO）训练模型，其奖励函数联合平衡精度、延迟和两种并行比率。推理时通过工具调用执行学习到的并行结构，将理论加速转化为实际墙钟加速。

Result: 在AIME24和AIME25等数学推理基准上的实验表明，Parason实现了平均约1.7倍的加速，同时保持有竞争力的精度。

Conclusion: 试验并行是LLM推理中一种重要且主导的并行形式，Parason通过识别、训练和利用两种并行形式，为实现高效推理加速提供了一条有前景的路径，在不明显牺牲精度的前提下显著缩短推理时间。

Abstract: Scaling test-time reasoning has substantially improved the problem-solving ability of large language models (LLMs), but standard autoregressive decoding still executes long reasoning traces sequentially, creating severe latency for difficult tasks (up to days and weeks). Parallel reasoning offers a natural remedy. However, prior systems primarily focus on Subtask Parallelism, where the model learns to decompose a high-level task into smaller chunks that can be solved independently. This approach overlooks another pervasive form of parallelism: Trial Parallelism, where multiple speculative attempts explore, verify, and aggregate competing hypotheses in parallel. In this paper, we introduce Parason, which reveals and learns both forms of parallelism in LLM reasoning. Our analysis identifies Trial Parallelism as the majority of parallelizable reasoning computation (65.5% in DeepSeek-V4's reasoning steps in HLE), and it becomes increasingly dominant on hard problems. Guided by this taxonomy, Parason converts sequential reasoning traces into structured parallel trajectories with a context-free grammar, then trains models with Parallelism-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization (PA-GRPO), whose reward jointly balances accuracy, latency, and the two parallelism ratios. At inference time, Parason executes the learned parallel structure through tool calls, translating theoretical savings to real-world wall-clock acceleration. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks including AIME24 and AIME25 show that Parason achieves an average acceleration about 1.7$\times$ while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [230] [The Invisible Editorial Layer: Formalizing Undisclosed Inference-Time Steering, Probability Placement, and the Attribution Problem in Deployed Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24662)
*Augusto Camargo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个被忽视的事实：LLM的可观察行为不仅由模型权重、训练数据和用户提示决定，还可能被推断管线中token选择之前施加的logit级干预系统性修改。作者提出"推断时框架偏差"概念，并引入三个核心概念：推断归因问题、概率放置和推断策略透明度，探讨了这些概念与欧盟AI法案、数字服务法案和FTC准则的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估假设模型行为主要由权重、训练数据和提示决定，忽略了现代推断管线可在token采样前系统性修改概率分布这一关键层。虽然受控生成和文本水印技术证明了logit级干预的技术成熟度，但其治理、安全和商业影响未被充分探讨，尤其是在政治、意识形态和商业框架的系统性倾向塑造方面。

Method: 作者采用概念性分析框架，形式化"模型≠部署系统"这一运营现实，提出三个原创概念：(1)推断归因问题——在有限可观测性下，行为偏差无法一般性地因果归因于模型权重；(2)概率放置——定义一种假设性广告原语，商业影响通过生成概率的系统性偏移实现而非显式产品插入；(3)推断策略透明度——使部署层干预可审计的治理原则。并将这些概念与欧盟AI法案第5条、DSA和FTC准则进行对照分析。

Result: 论文构建了一个概念性框架，论证推断时干预构成一个独立的治理关注域：它既不能归因于模型本身，又可能被商业或意识形态利益方利用，同时现有监管框架（欧盟AI法案、DSA、FTC）尚未充分覆盖这一层级的干预。研究建立了从技术现状（受控生成、水印）到政策缺口（透明度要求）的系统性论证。

Conclusion: 推断管线中的logit级干预已具备技术成熟度，需将其视为独立于模型权重的治理层面。作者倡导建立"推断策略透明度"作为新的治理原则，以确保部署层的系统性概率修改可被审计和追责，这对用户的知情决策和监管机构的有效执法至关重要。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are commonly evaluated under the assumption that their observable behavior is primarily determined by model weights, training data, alignment procedures, and user prompts. This view is incomplete. Modern inference pipelines may systematically modify the probability distribution produced by a model immediately before token selection, creating an additional layer of control between frozen weights and observed text.
  While controlled generation (e.g., PPLM, GeDi, DExperts, FUDGE) and text-watermarking systems (e.g., SynthID-Text) demonstrate the technical maturity of decoding- and logit-level interventions, the governance, security, and economic implications of an undisclosed inference policy remain comparatively underexplored. This paper examines the emergence of inference-time framing bias: the systematic modification of generated language toward political, ideological, institutional, or commercial frames via interventions applied after model inference but before token sampling.
  We formalize the operational reality Model != Deployed System and introduce three concepts: (1) the Inference Attribution Problem, characterizing why observed behavioral bias cannot generally be causally attributed to model weights alone under limited observability; (2) Probability Placement, defining a hypothetical advertising primitive in which commercial influence is implemented through systematic shifts in generation probabilities rather than explicit product insertions; and (3) Inference Policy Transparency, a governance principle for making deployment-layer interventions auditable. We examine these concepts in relation to Article 5 of the EU AI Act, the EU Digital Services Act, and FTC doctrines.

</details>


### [231] [Confident at the moment of action: belief miscalibration in LLM play under hidden information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24691)
*Bhushan Kashinath Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在隐藏信息的国际象棋变体中测试智能体自述置信度与决策正确性的一致性，发现高置信度（≥0.5）下对隐藏皇家棋子位置的捕捉正确率仅为1/62，校准缺陷几乎全部集中在这些高置信事件中，且通过不同模型配置复现了该模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统日益依赖模型自身陈述的置信度来门控决策，这隐含一个假设：置信度在行动时刻与正确性相关。作者希望检验这一假设是否成立，因为如果置信度与正确性脱节，基于置信度的安全门控机制将失效。

Method: 使用隐藏信息国际象棋变体，皇家状态可以在棋子间秘密且重复地转移。每一轮分别向智能体询问其对对手隐藏皇家棋子位置的置信度分布（独立于其选择的落子），赛后根据可恢复的ground truth对置信度进行打分。实验跨越两个独立批次及多个模型配置（涵盖两家提供商），并在独立座位中对比传统评价轴与信念质量。

Result: 高置信度（≥0.5）下捕捉正确的比例仅1/62；校准缺陷集中在这些事件中（原批次99.3%，复制批次98.7%）。同样的模式在更弱的程度上在四个额外模型配置中一致排序（仅为点估计，多数差异统计上不可区分）。一个固定外部榜单得分的同模型对比显示，仅改变推理预算就能使指标移动幅度接近大型跨模型差距。在另一座位中，合法性、成本、延迟、完成率等传统评价轴可与信念质量完全分离，所有传统轴最优的配置反而产生最差的信念质量。

Conclusion: 模型的自述置信度在关键时刻可能与正确性严重脱节，且这种脱节无法通过传统结果导向的评价检测到——表现出该模式的模型仍能赢得其信念所针对的游戏，因此仅看胜负等结果指标会遗漏校准失败。

Abstract: Agentic systems increasingly gate actions on a model's own stated confidence, which assumes confidence tracks correctness at the moment of acting. We test this in a hidden-information chess variant where royal status can be secretly, repeatedly relocated between pieces, and where an agent's stated probability distribution over the opponent's hidden royal piece -- elicited every turn, separately from the move it chooses -- is scored against ground truth recoverable after the game. Across two independent batches, captures made at high stated confidence ($\geq 0.5$) about the hidden piece's location were correct in 1 of 62 cases. The calibration deficit is concentrated almost entirely in these events: 99.3% of it in the original batch, 98.7% in the replication. The same pattern, in weaker form, orders consistently (point estimates only; most pairwise gaps are not statistically distinguishable at this sample size) across four further model configurations spanning a second provider -- reported as scope for the finding, not as evidence that capability predicts calibration: a same-model comparison at a fixed external leaderboard score shows a deliberation-budget change alone moves the metric by nearly as much as a large cross-model gap. In a separate seat, conventional evaluation axes -- legality, cost, latency, completion rate -- can dissociate entirely from belief quality, with the configuration winning on every conventional axis producing the worst belief quality tested. A model exhibiting this pattern can still win the game its belief was about, which is why outcome-only evaluation would not detect it.

</details>


### [232] [Lifted Model Construction under Approximate Commutativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24713)
*Malte Luttermann,Jan Speller,Tanya Braun,Marcel Gehrke,Ralf Möller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文引入ε-交换性概念，放宽了提升推理中因子交换性的严格条件，允许参数学习产生的近似交换因子被用于提升模型构建和概率推理，并提供严格的近似误差上界保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升推理（Lifted Inference）依赖于识别可交换因子来构建提升表示，但实际中从数据学习的参数往往只能保证因子近似交换而非严格交换，导致提升方法难以应用于真实场景。

Method: 提出ε-交换性的形式化定义，作为传统交换性的松弛版本；展示如何利用ε-交换性进行提升模型构建和下游概率推理；推导近似误差的严格上界。

Result: 理论证明ε-交换性可确保提升模型构建的实用可行性，同时保持高精度查询结果；实验验证了在更低运行时间下实现了可比的查询精度。

Conclusion: ε-交换性有效弥合了理论假设与实际数据之间的差距，使提升推理在实际参数化场景中保持高精度和高效率。

Abstract: Lifted inference algorithms enable scalable probabilistic inference even for large object domains by leveraging the indistinguishability of objects in a probability distribution. An essential prerequisite for constructing a lifted representation is to identify commutative factors, i.e., functions whose output values are invariant under permutations of a subset of their input values, in a potential-based factorisation. In practice, however, parameters learned from data inevitably deviate even if associated objects are indistinguishable, causing their corresponding factors to be only approximately commutative instead of being exactly commutative. We address this problem by introducing the concept of ε-commutativity, a relaxation of commutativity where output values are only approximately invariant under permutations of input values. Specifically, we show how ε-commutativity can be exploited for lifted model construction, downstream probabilistic inference, and prove strict bounds on the induced approximation error, thereby ensuring the practical applicability of lifted model construction while maintaining highly accurate query results. These theoretical guarantees are confirmed empirically, demonstrating comparable query accuracy at lower runtime.

</details>


### [233] [Meta$^n$: Recursive Self-Improvement through Emergent Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24735)
*Zae Myung Kim,Young-Jun Lee,Seungyeon Jwa,Dongyeop Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Meta^n，一种新型自改进LLM代理方法。核心思想是固定元操作Ω，递归地将Ω应用于其自身的产物（每次读取当前求解器栈的轨迹和产生它们的代码，然后写出下一层作为策略预处理和可调用辅助函数库）。因为Ω不变，系统保持稳定；因为输入严格增长，每一层都比上一层具有更高的推理视角。深度由收敛决定而非预先设定，进化存档用于搜索层链。在两个主干模型上，Meta^n在全部八个基准族上超越先前自改进代理，尤其在ARC-AGI-2上独占得分超过零的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自改进LLM代理存在根本局限：它们改进答案而非产生答案的过程；添加元层会固定该层从而限制元深度（约两层）；自编辑系统必须留一部分机制不改动以保持稳定，这从结构上限制了自改进的深度和通用性。作者希望突破这一元深度上限。

Method: 提出Meta^n架构：固定一个元操作Ω，Ω反复作用于自己的产物。每次应用Ω时，它读取下方求解器栈的轨迹以及生成这些轨迹的代码，然后输出下一层——包含一个战略预处理模块和一个可调用的辅助函数库。因Ω固定不变，系统不会不稳定；因输入不断增大，每层拥有更高的推理视点。深度通过收敛准则动态确定，并用进化算法在层链空间中搜索。

Result: 在两种主干模型上，Meta^n在全部八个基准族上优于先前自改进代理。最突出的是ARC-AGI-2（专为抵抗技能记忆而设计），Meta^n是唯一得分超过零的方法。消融实验表明递归带来的大部分增益来自每层传递给下一层的条件化信息，且随深度增加会涌现出不同的层角色（尽管没有提示词规定这一分工）。

Conclusion: Meta^n通过固定的元操作配合不断增长的输入，打破了自改进系统的元深度瓶颈（约两层），实现了更高阶、更可扩展的自改进能力。其成功的关键在于"操作固定、输入递归"，并显示出深度化自改进能自然涌现功能分化。代码已开源。

Abstract: Self-improving LLM agents refine answers, not the process that produces those answers. Systems that add a meta-level hold that level fixed, and those that edit themselves must leave part of their own editing machinery untouched to stay stable, capping the meta-depth they realize at roughly two. We present Meta$^n$, which keeps the meta-operation fixed and recurses on its input instead. That operation, $Ω$, is applied repeatedly to its own products, reading the traces of the solver stack below together with the code that produced them, then writing the next layer as a strategic pre-process and a library of callable helpers. Because $Ω$ never changes, it cannot destabilize the system, and because its input strictly grows, each layer reasons from a higher vantage than the last. Depth is set by convergence rather than fixed in advance, and an evolutionary archive searches over layer chains. Across two backbones, Meta$^n$ outperforms prior self-improving agents on all eight benchmark families. The sharpest case is ARC-AGI-2, built to resist skill memorization, where it alone scores above zero. Ablations indicate that most of the gain from recursion comes from the conditioning each layer passes to the next, and distinct layer roles emerge with depth although no prompt prescribes them. Code available at https://github.com/minnesotanlp/meta-n

</details>


### [234] [RACE: Scalable Statistical Estimation of Functional Consistency in LLM Neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24758)
*Runyu Wang,Bo Liu,Xiaxin Zhang,Yu Han,Jiawei Cao,Xiaoye Zhang,Zhe Zhang,Yifan Yang,Peng Ping*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RACE框架，一种基于前向传播的统计方法，用于评估Transformer神经元在整域范围内的功能一致性，相较梯度方法具有更高的域特异性和低两个数量级的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性中，发现跨整个数据域的稳定神经元行为仍然具有挑战性。现有方法要么依赖实例级别的点估计而掩盖了群体层面的可变性，要么依赖计算昂贵的梯度方法而限制了可扩展的域范围分析。

Method: RACE（Residual Alignment for Consistency Estimation）是一种基于前向传播的统计框架，通过残差对齐来评估Transformer神经元的域级功能一致性，无需反向传播或梯度计算。

Result: 扰动实验表明RACE相比基于梯度的点估计方法具有更好的域特异性；词元分布级别的结果验证了所选神经元与目标域的关联性；计算开销比梯度方法低两个数量级。

Conclusion: RACE提供了一种高效且域特异的统计方案，能够规模化地进行跨域神经元功能一致性分析，弥补了现有方法在群体可变性刻画与计算可扩展性方面的不足。

Abstract: Discovering stable neuron behavior across entire domains remains a challenge in mechanistic interpretability. Existing methods often rely on instance-level point estimates or computationally expensive procedures, which either obscure population-level variability or limit scalable domain-wide analysis. We present RACE (Residual Alignment for Consistency Estimation), a forward-pass statistical framework that evaluates the domain-wide functional consistency of Transformer neurons. Perturbation experiments demonstrate that RACE achieves superior domain specificity compared to gradient-based point estimates. Meanwhile, token-distribution-level results verify the association between the selected neurons and the target domain. Furthermore, its computational overhead is two orders of magnitude lower than that of gradient-based methods.

</details>


### [235] [Evidence Blindness in Direct Corpus Interaction: Persistent Navigation with AtlasNav](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24764)
*Hongyu Guo,Zhiyu Zheng,Zhao Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AtlasNav，一种持久化多视图语料库导航框架，用于大规模智能体检索。传统检索增强生成向直接语料库交互（DCI）范式演进，但有限交互预算下证据可能逐步静默丢失（作者称为"证据盲视"）。AtlasNav将语料库一次性组织为Corpus Atlas，使每个查询自适应导航而非在线重建共享结构。在BrowseComp-Plus上达到92.05%严格准确率，在线推理成本降低30.21%，在PhantomWiki及异构企业知识库上均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DCI范式下，智能体直接与完整语料库交互，但有限交互预算下证据可能逐步静默丢失：所需证据可能无法浮现、浮现的证据文档可能未被打开、打开后可能无法暴露关键片段。作者将这一渐进式静默损失称为"证据盲视"。同时，现有方法（动态工作区方法）从每个查询和轨迹重建查询条件化的交互空间，结构恢复主要在线进行，缺乏可复用的语料库组织，导致重复计算和效率低下。因此作者提出将大规模智能体检索形式化为有限预算下对可复用语料库结构的导航问题。

Method: AtlasNav是一个持久化多视图语料库导航框架。其核心思想是保留直接语料库交互能力，但预先一次性将语料库组织为Corpus Atlas（持久化的多视图结构），使每个查询可以在该结构上进行自适应导航，而非从零重建共享结构。该框架通过阶段级证据实现（stage-wise evidence realization）量化证据盲视，并基于有限的交互预算优化导航路径，使完整证据能更早被实现。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus基准上，AtlasNav达到92.05%的严格准确率，同时相比先前最优的动态工作区方法将记录在案的在线推理成本降低30.21%。在匹配预算下，它能更早实现完整所需证据，并更快逼近同一模型在证据加持下的经验参考。该表示原则在PhantomWiki的不同语料库组织及10K-1M规模控制扩展下同样有效，并能竞争性地迁移到异构企业知识场景。

Conclusion: 智能体检索不仅取决于证据的可达性，还取决于语料库的表示方式——即如何在有限的交互预算下将交互转化为有效的导航。通过持久化的多视图语料库组织（Corpus Atlas），可以实现更高效的证据实现和更优的准确率-成本权衡，这为大规模智能体检索提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Large language model agents are moving beyond conventional retrieval-augmented generation toward direct interaction with external corpora. Direct Corpus Interaction (DCI) keeps the full corpus accessible, yet reachable evidence can remain unusable under finite interaction budgets. Required evidence may fail to surface, a surfaced supporting document may remain unopened, or an opened document may fail to expose its decisive fragment. We call this progressive silent loss Evidence Blindness and quantify it through stage-wise evidence realization. Within the DCI paradigm, raw interaction adds little reusable corpus organization, while dynamic-workspace methods reconstruct a query-conditioned interaction space from each query and trajectory. In both cases, useful structure is recovered largely online. We instead formulate large-scale agentic search as finite-budget navigation over reusable corpus structure. We introduce AtlasNav, a persistent multi-view corpus-navigation framework that retains direct corpus interaction but organizes the corpus once into a Corpus Atlas, allowing each query to navigate adaptively rather than reconstruct shared structure. On BrowseComp-Plus, AtlasNav achieves 92.05% strict accuracy while reducing recorded online inference cost by 30.21% relative to the prior dynamic-workspace state of the art. Under matched budgets, it realizes the complete required evidence earlier and approaches the same model's evidence-supplied empirical reference more rapidly. The same representation principle remains effective under PhantomWiki's distinct corpus organization and controlled 10K-1M scaling, and transfers competitively to heterogeneous enterprise knowledge. These results show that agentic search depends not only on accessible evidence, but also on how the corpus is represented so that limited interaction becomes effective navigation.

</details>


### [236] [StepGuard: Learning Step-Level Guardrails with Scalable Supervision and Safety-Utility Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24777)
*Zhijie Zheng,Yu Li,Chen Qian,Yuqian Fu,Yanwei Fu,Lu Sheng,Jing Shao,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出StepGuard，一个步骤级防护模型，可在工具动作执行前进行审计，并配以自动数据引擎StepGen生成安全/不安全轨迹对，以及Balance-GRPO平衡策略减少过度防御与防御不足。实验表明其在开放权重防护模型中准确率最高，可将攻击成功率降低77.3%而效用仅下降2.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体通过工具调用与外部环境交互，但这一能力引入了安全风险（如文件修改、信息泄露、未授权操作）。现有防护机制通常只评估完整轨迹，对执行前步骤级动作的监控研究不足，存在安全盲区。

Method: 提出StepGuard步骤级防护模型，可审计已完成轨迹并在工具动作执行前进行校验。训练数据由StepGen自动数据引擎生成，该引擎在相同上下文下生成仅在风险步骤处动作不同的安全与不安全轨迹对。同时提出Balance-GRPO，根据安全/不安全动作的观测准确率动态平衡两者学习，以减少过度防御与防御不足。

Result: StepGuard在开放权重防护模型中取得最高平均准确率，性能与GPT-5.4相当。用于守护AgentDojo和AgentDyn上的智能体时，相对于无防护设置平均攻击成功率降低77.3%，平均效用仅下降2.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 步骤级执行前防护是可行的且高效的，StepGuard在显著降低安全风险的同时仅带来极小的效用损失，验证了该范式在实际智能体防护中的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based agents can interact with external environments through tool invocation, but this capability also introduces security risks such as file modification, information leakage, and unauthorized actions. Existing guardrails often evaluate completed trajectories, leaving pre-execution monitoring of step-level actions underexplored. We propose StepGuard, a step-level guard model that can audit completed agent trajectories and check tool actions before they are executed. To train StepGuard, we introduce StepGen, an automatic data engine that generates safe and unsafe trajectories with the same context but different actions at the risky step. To further reduce over-defense and under-defense, we propose Balance-GRPO, which dynamically balances learning between safe and unsafe actions based on their observed accuracy. Experiments show that StepGuard achieves the highest average accuracy among open-weight guard models, with performance comparable to GPT-5.4. When used to guard agents on AgentDojo and AgentDyn, StepGuard reduces mean attack success rate by 77.3% relative to the no-guard setting, while mean utility drops by only 2.8 percentage points.

</details>


### [237] [Right Diagnoses, Decorative Reasoning:A Perturbation Audit of Medical Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24790)
*Mengzhu Xu,Jifan Gao,Xia Jiang,Yaoxin Wu,Xi Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种医学领域的思维链（CoT）忠实性审计框架，通过30种临床动机关联的扰动算子编辑CoT和问题，并配合"链更新×答案翻转"联合分析，发现14个LLM在四个医学QA基准上的Chain-Decoupling Rate（CDR）高达72.9%，即大多数模型的CoT并未真正参与答案推理，删除或篡改CoT不影响准确性。两位临床医生验证97%以上的扰动问题黄金答案依然成立，表明医学CoT更多是"文档"而非"推理证据"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床医生会将CoT推理过程视为医学推理的证据，但现有通用领域的CoT忠实性探针忽略了临床代价，而医学LLM评估又把CoT当作黑箱处理。论文旨在弥合这一空白，提供专门针对医学场景的CoT忠实性审计方法。

Method: 提出医学扰动审计（medical perturbation audit）：构建30种临床动机扰动算子，同时编辑CoT和问题（包括严重性反转、否定翻转、人口学属性交换、证据删减等），并设计链更新×答案翻转的联合分析对每个模型按失效模式分类。对14个LLM在四个医学QA基准上应用该框架。

Result: 三个独立测试结论收敛：1）CDR在临床有意义破坏性编辑上全panel平均72.9%；2）CoT损坏后准确率不变；3）移除CoT提示不降低准确率。两位执业临床医生重新标注197个扰动问题，98.5%的黄金答案仍然成立。该模式在医学和推理微调及不同规模下一致，闭源模型中仅凭答案侧信号也观察到同样的解耦现象。

Conclusion: 医学领域的CoT在很大程度上并未被模型实际用于推理，而是作为"文档"存在。论文的框架和CDR指标为审计医学CoT是否忠实提供了可复用的度量标准，提示医生与监管者不应把CoT文本视为模型真实推理过程的可靠证据。

Abstract: Clinicians read chain-of-thought (CoT) rationales as evidence of medical reasoning, but whether the visible chain plays that role is rarely tested. General-domain CoT-faithfulness probes ignore clinical cost, and medical LLM evaluations treat the chain as a black box. We close this gap with a medical perturbation audit: a 30-operator battery edits both the chain and the question with clinically motivated operators (severity reversal, negation flip, demographic swap, evidence ablation), paired with a chain-update times answer-flip joint analysis that classifies each model by its failure mode. Applied to 14 LLMs on four medical QA benchmarks, three independent tests converge: the Chain-Decoupling Rate (CDR; chain does not register the edit and the answer does not flip) is 72.9% panel-wide on clinically meaningful destructive edits, chain corruption leaves accuracy unchanged, and removing CoT prompting does not reduce accuracy. Two board-certified clinicians re-annotate N=197 perturbed questions; 98.5% leave the gold defensible. The pattern holds across medical and reasoning fine-tuning and scale; on the closed-source tier, where the chain text is unavailable, the answer-side signals are consistent with the same decoupling. Our framework and CDR provide a reusable yardstick for auditing whether medical CoT is faithful or merely documentation.

</details>


### [238] [CAFE: Self-Improving Search Agents Need Co-Evolving Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24794)
*Boyang Liu,Senjie Jin,Peixin Wang,Zhangyue Yin,Yibo Wang,Yuhao Zhou,Xinbing Liang,Shizheng Zhu,Yuhui Wang,Jingqi Tong,Zhiheng Xi,Jiazheng Zhang,Clive Bai,Clarenceai,Blaze Chen,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAFE（耦合智能体-反馈演进）框架通过共享参数模型交替扮演搜索智能体和批评者角色，将在线强化学习与离线偏好优化耦合，使反馈随策略共同演进，在七个智能体搜索基准上优于现有RL搜索方法，并显著减少幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结果监督的搜索智能体虽能学习何时检索及如何检索证据，但终端奖励既无法定位中间错误，也无法在错误累积前重定向轨迹。批评者从结果混淆的轨迹中推断纠正性反馈面临困难，因为失败模式会随智能体改进而改变。需要一种让反馈与策略共同演进的机制。

Method: CAFE框架采用共享参数的模型交替扮演搜索智能体与批评者角色。包含三个核心组件：（1）基于基座智能体自身失败的轨迹初始化反馈条件恢复（2）在线RL阶段，通过提示级别的调用-跳过成功差距构建比较性反馈估计来塑造请求回报，并使用反馈感知优势塑造对反馈前后的token优势进行重新加权（3）离线阶段，从匹配的成功与失败轨迹中通过轨迹派生的偏好优化学习反馈。

Result: 在七个智能体搜索基准上，CAFE平均表现优于所评估的RL搜索方法；在全部六个域外基准上保持增益；有效减少答案层面的幻觉。单侧消融实验表明仅改进智能体或仅改进批评者最终会陷入平台期，而交替更新两者则持续提升性能。

Conclusion: 自改进的搜索智能体需要与它所引导的策略共同演进的反馈。批评者与智能体的耦合式交替优化是实现持续性能提升的关键，单侧优化均存在瓶颈。

Abstract: Outcome-supervised search agents learn when and how to retrieve evidence, but terminal rewards neither localize intermediate errors nor redirect an ongoing trajectory before those errors compound. Treating corrective feedback as a learned in-trajectory intervention couples the two roles: the agent must decide when to request and use feedback, while the critic must infer useful corrections from outcome-confounded rollouts whose failure patterns shift as the agent improves. We introduce CAFE (Coupled Agent--Feedback Evolution), a framework in which a shared-parameter model alternates between search-agent and critic roles. CAFE initializes feedback-conditioned recovery from trajectories built around the base agent's own failures, then couples online and offline optimization. During online RL, a comparative feedback estimate uses a prompt-level call--skip success gap to shape request returns, while feedback-aware advantage shaping reweights token advantages before and after feedback. Offline, rollout-derived preference optimization learns feedback from matched successful and unsuccessful trajectories. On seven agentic search benchmarks, CAFE outperforms the evaluated RL-based search agents on average, retains its gains across all six out-of-domain benchmarks, and reduces answer-level hallucinations. One-sided ablations show that improving only the agent or only the critic eventually plateaus, whereas alternating the two updates continues to improve performance. These findings suggest that a self-improving search agent needs feedback that co-evolves with the policy it guides.

</details>


### [239] [StarHarness: Evolving Harnesses with Stratified Search for Enterprise Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24804)
*Esakkivel Esakkiraja,Denis Akhiyarov,Vikas Yadav,Sai Rajeswar,Patrice Bechard,Sridhar Nemala,Sagar Davasam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StarHarness是一个框架，在保持模型权重不变的前提下，为特定环境演化智能体（agent）的"束具"（harness），包括提示语、工具接口、技能、子智能体结构和循环配置等。通过在三个企业基准（ITBench SRE、EnterpriseOps-Gym ITSM、AutomationBench Finance）上的实验，束具演化使全基准性能提升20-35个百分点，且这些收益可泛化到未参与演化的任务，并跨GPT和Qwen模型家族迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工具丰富的企业任务中，预训练模型与具体运行环境之间存在持续性的"模型-环境失配"问题，这种失配往往被归因于模型能力不足而非环境适配问题。作者认为可以通过演化环境特定的智能体束具（而非重新训练模型权重）来缩小这一差距，从而提供一种更经济实用的解决方案。

Method: StarHarness框架包含三个关键设计：(1) 构建紧凑的演化池，通过基线失败行为对任务进行分层；(2) 将提议者可见的搜索任务与提议者隐藏的选择任务分离；(3) 保留未参与演化的任务用于评估泛化能力。演化范围覆盖提示语与任务框架、工具接口、技能、MCP支持的服务提供者、子智能体结构以及agent循环配置。

Result: 在三个企业环境中，经过每环境4-12次被接受的改动，全基准性能提升20-35个百分点。这些收益在演化过程中未涉及的held-out任务上仍然保持，且无需重新演化即可跨GPT和Qwen模型家族迁移。轨迹分析显示改进主要来自接口修复、环境约定和操作知识的压缩，在多个场景中减少了误报诊断并缩短了轨迹长度。

Conclusion: StarHarness提供了一种实用的路径来减少工具丰富的企业任务中持久的模型-环境失配问题，表明通过演化环境特定的智能体束具可以在不触碰模型权重的情况下获得显著的性能增益，且具备良好的泛化与跨模型迁移特性。

Abstract: We present StarHarness, a framework for evolving environment-specific agent harnesses while keeping model weights fixed. The evolved harness can include prompt and task framing, tool interfaces, skills, MCP-backed providers, subagent structure, and agent-loop configuration. StarHarness constructs a compact evolution pool by stratifying tasks according to baseline failure behavior, separates proposer-visible search tasks from proposer-hidden selection tasks, and reserves held-out tasks for evaluating generalization. Across ITBench SRE, EnterpriseOps-Gym ITSM, and AutomationBench Finance, harness evolution improves full-benchmark performance by 20-35 percentage points over the default harness after 4-12 accepted changes per environment. These gains persist on tasks excluded from evolution and transfer without re-evolution across GPT and Qwen model families. Trace analysis links the improvements to interface repairs, environment conventions, and operational knowledge that compresses search, with fewer false-positive diagnoses and shorter trajectories in several settings. StarHarness therefore offers a practical way to reduce persistent model-environment mismatch in tool-rich enterprise tasks.

</details>


### [240] [Strictly Causal Streaming Video Anomaly Detection with a Theoretically-Grounded State-Space Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24810)
*Yogesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种严格因果的流式视频异常检测器，基于对角线性状态空间递归（带输入与状态相关的衰减门控），以O(1)时间和内存逐帧更新固定大小状态，无回看和片段缓冲。理论上推导了递归衰减谱与检测延迟及可捕获最短异常之间的封闭关系，并在UCSD Ped2和CUHK Avenue上实证验证。在Apple M3 Pro硬件上实测端到端延迟0.74-0.77ms/帧（超1300 FPS），帧级AUC为67.9%和70.2%，落后于先前非因果SSM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Mamba风格状态空间模型的视频异常检测方法仍依赖内部剪辑/窗口缓冲，缺乏对时序记忆与检测延迟关系的理论解释，且仅用GPU吞吐量而非目标边缘硬件进行效率基准测试。作者希望构建一个真正流式、无缓冲、具备理论延迟保证、并在实际边缘硬件上评测的异常检测器。

Method: 采用严格因果的流式检测器：固定大小状态逐帧O(1)更新，无需lookahead和clip缓冲。时序核心为带输入与状态相关衰减门的对角线性状态空间递归，在冻结视觉骨干上通过因果下一嵌入预测进行自监督训练。理论上推导了递归衰减谱与检测延迟及最短可捕获异常间的封闭关系，并在Apple M3 Pro上实测延迟与吞吐量。通过消融实验分析衰减率、状态大小和门控的影响。

Result: 理论上预测的settling延迟界（基于学习得到的base decay为57-59帧）远高于实测检测延迟（1.6和18.4帧），说明事件边界门控而非base decay主导响应速度。Apple M3 Pro上端到端延迟0.74-0.77ms/帧（超1300 FPS）。帧级AUC在Ped2和Avenue上为67.9%与70.2%，落后于非因果SSM基线。消融显示门控贡献依赖数据集规模：在小规模Ped2训练集上有损精度，在较大Avenue上有益。

Conclusion: 该流式检测器在效率和理论可解释性上显著优于现有方案，但精度低于非因果基线；门控机制对响应速度起主导作用而base decay主要影响settling时间。下一步需缩小精度差距并扩展到第三个更大规模的基准上进行验证。

Abstract: Recent work has applied Mamba style state space models (SSMs) to video anomaly detection, yet existing approaches still rely on buffering clips or windows internally, lack a theoretical account of how temporal memory relates to detection latency, and benchmark efficiency only through GPU throughput rather than the edge hardware these methods are intended to target. We introduce a strictly causal streaming anomaly detector whose fixed size state is updated in O(1) time and memory per incoming frame, with no lookahead and no clip buffering. Its temporal core is a diagonal linear state space recurrence with an input and state dependent decay gate, trained self supervised through causal next embedding prediction on a frozen visual backbone. We derive a closed form relationship between the recurrence decay spectrum and both detection delay and the shortest anomaly it can reliably capture, then validate empirically on UCSD Ped2 and CUHK Avenue. The settling delay bound predicted from the learned base decay (57 to 59 frames) sits far above the measured detection delay (1.6 and 18.4 frames), showing that the event boundary gate, not the base decay, governs responsiveness. We further report end to end latency and throughput measured directly on Apple M3 Pro hardware, 0.74 ms and 0.77 ms per frame (over 1300 FPS), rather than simulated GPU numbers. With an untuned initial configuration the method reaches 67.9 percent and 70.2 percent frame level AUC on Ped2 and Avenue, trailing prior non causal SSM baselines in accuracy. Ablations over decay rate, state size, and gating reveal that the gate contribution is dataset size dependent, hurting accuracy on the smaller Ped2 training set but helping on the larger Avenue one. Closing this accuracy gap and extending evaluation to a third, larger benchmark are immediate next steps.

</details>


### [241] [Constrained Entity Selection under Partial Knowledge for LLM-Based Knowledge Graph QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24824)
*Emanuel Kitzelmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CES-PK问题框架，通过轻量级符号约束对LLM生成的候选答案进行验证，采用三值约束语义（满足/违反/未知）处理不完整知识图谱，在Hetionet生物医学KG上实验表明该方法能提升精度并保持召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在知识图谱问答（KGQA）中常无法正确将答案锚定到图上。现有方法要么依赖将问题完全解析为SPARQL等可执行查询（对复杂schema或不完整KG脆弱），要么依赖LLM在KG上进行推理生成答案（更鲁棒但缺乏形式化保证）。需要一个既能提供形式化保证又能适应真实KG不完整性的折中方案。

Method: 提出Constrained Entity Selection under Partial Knowledge (CES-PK)问题框架。核心思想是：由LLM系统生成候选答案，然后利用从问题中提取的轻量级符号约束（类型、关系、排除约束）进行验证。为避免不完整KG导致的错误拒绝，采用三值约束语义（satisfied, violated, unknown）。在Hetionet生物医学KG上实例化该框架，评估三种约束类型的效果。

Result: 实验表明：通过过滤无效候选答案可提升精度；由于保留未被明确违反约束的候选，召回率得以保持；满足的约束可作为额外的正向符号证据，用于对剩余候选进行排序。

Conclusion: CES-PK填补了完全语义解析与无约束LLM推理之间的空白，在无需构建可执行逻辑形式的前提下，通过轻量级符号约束为LLM生成的KGQA答案提供形式化验证，兼顾了鲁棒性和可验证性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for knowledge graph question answering (KGQA), but can fail to correctly ground answers in the underlying graph. Current approaches to LLM-based KGQA either rely on full semantic parsing into executable queries such as SPARQL, which is brittle in practice due to complex schemas or incompleteness of real-world KGs, or on LLM-reasoning and answer generation over KGs, which can be more robust but lacks formal guarantees. In this work, we study a complementary setting in which \emph{candidate} answers are generated by an LLM-based system and subsequently verified using lightweight symbolic constraints derived from the question. We introduce \emph{Constrained Entity Selection under Partial Knowledge (CES-PK)}, a problem formulation that focuses on eliminating invalid answers and providing symbolic support for valid ones without requiring construction of executable logical forms. To account for incomplete KGs, we employ a three-valued constraint semantics (\emph{satisfied, violated, unknown}) that avoids incorrect rejections under open-world assumptions. To demonstrate the effects of our method, we instantiate this framework over the Hetionet biomedical knowledge graph and evaluate the impact of type, relation, and exclusion constraints. Experiments show that precision improves by filtering invalid candidates, while recall is preserved due to retaining candidates whose constraints are not explicitly violated. Satisfied constraints provide additional positive symbolic evidence to rank remaining candidates.

</details>


### [242] [A Dual-Dimensional LLM Framework for Automated Item Incidental Content Similarity Analysis in Large-Scale Assessments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24825)
*Jing Huang,Jihong Zhang,Hua-Hua Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于大语言模型(LLM)的双维度自动试题相似性分析框架(AISA)，通过结构化分解与语义关联两个维度量化试题的偶然性内容冗余。心理测量学验证显示LLM导出的指标比传统文本指标(BLEU/余弦相似度)更能对齐构念无关的局部依赖，且在计算机自适应测试(CAT)模拟中，基于LLM相似度约束的选题策略改善了估计稳定性并降低了偏差，仅需极小的效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模测评的扩张和自动试题生成的普及使得试题间内容冗余(如措辞、情境框架等构念无关元素)日益严重。传统相似度指标如BLEU或余弦相似度无法同时捕捉驱动感知冗余的细微结构与语义层面。

Method: 提出AISA框架，将试题相似性操作化为两个维度：结构化分解(Structured Decomposition)和语义关联(Semantic Relatedness)，并由LLM驱动实现。通过心理测量学验证对比LLM指标与传统文本指标，并在CAT模拟中将LLM相似度约束纳入试题选择策略进行评估。

Result: LLM导出的相似度指标与传统文本测量相比，与构念无关局部依赖指标的对齐程度更高，产生的试题参数分组更一致。CAT模拟显示，基于LLM相似度约束的选择策略在估计稳定性和偏差减少方面优于基于传统指标的策略，且效率折损极小。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的AISA框架在可扩展题库管理、内容感知的测试组卷以及对体验敏感的自适应测试方面具有显著潜力，可适用于多样化的测评场景。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of large-scale assessments and the growing adoption of automatic item generation have intensified concerns about incidental content redundancy, where construct-irrelevant elements such as wording or contextual framing become unintentionally repetitive across items. Traditional similarity metrics like BLEU or cosine similarity, often fail to capture the nuanced structural and semantic layers that drive perceived redundancy simultaneously. This study proposes a dual-dimensional framework for Automated Item Similarity Analysis (AISA) powered by Large Language Models (LLMs), operationalizing similarity through Structured Decomposition and Semantic Relatedness. Psychometric validation indicates that LLM-derived metrics align more closely with indicators of construct-irrelevant local dependence and yield more coherent item parameter groupings than traditional text-based measures. The framework is further evaluated through its application in Computerized Adaptive Testing (CAT). Simulations reveal that incorporating LLM-based similarity constraints into item selection improves estimation stability and reduces bias with minimal efficiency trade-offs, outperforming constraints based on conventional metrics. These findings highlight the potential of LLM-powered AISA to support scalable bank curation, content-aware test assembly, and experience-sensitive adaptive testing across diverse assessment contexts.

</details>


### [243] [FedV-KGQA: Multi-Hop Question Answering over Vertically Partitioned Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24846)
*Md Saikat Islam Khan Bappy,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FedV-KGQA是一个针对知识图谱问答的联邦学习框架，解决跨组织垂直分区知识图谱上的多跳推理问题。该方法允许各组织共享实体但拥有不相交的关系集合，通过本地图增强和知识图谱嵌入建立结构数据边界，无需运行时跨孤岛通信即可将问题锚定到正确的图邻域。在三个基准上的12种模型配置评估表明，该方法性能接近集中式系统，可泛化到3跳推理并对嵌入扰动具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中知识图谱问答数据常因治理和数据主权约束而分散在不同组织中。集中式系统无法回答所需事实分布在垂直分区孤岛上的多跳问题，因此需要一个能够在保护各组织数据隐私的前提下完成跨孤岛多跳推理的框架。

Method: 提出FedV-KGQA框架，核心包括：(1)本地图增强与知识图谱嵌入相结合，确保原始三元组和关系参数不离开各孤岛，建立结构数据边界；(2)无需集中式图访问；(3)引入主题实体锚定机制，在无运行时跨孤岛通信的情况下将问题正确锚定到图邻域。

Result: 在三个基准上评估了12种模型配置，结果表明FedV-KGQA性能强劲，接近集中式系统性能，能够泛化到3跳推理，并对嵌入扰动具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FedV-KGQA是首个能够在垂直分区知识图谱上进行多跳推理的联邦框架，在不牺牲性能的前提下实现了结构数据边界和隐私保护，为跨组织知识图谱问答提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Real-world data for knowledge graph question answering is often distributed across different organizations due to governance and data sovereignty constraints. While centralized systems exist, they cannot answer multi-hop questions when the required facts are split across vertically partitioned silos. In this paper, we propose FedV-KGQA, a framework for multi-hop reasoning over knowledge graphs in which organizations share entities but own disjoint sets of relations. Our approach combines local graph enrichment and knowledge graph embeddings to ensure raw triples and relation parameters never leave each silo, establishing a structural data boundary without requiring centralized graph access. We further introduce a topic entity anchoring mechanism that grounds questions in the correct graph neighborhood without any runtime inter-silo communication. We evaluate 12 model configurations across three benchmarks and show that FedV-KGQA performs strongly, remains close to centralized performance, generalizes to 3-hop reasoning, and is robust to embedding perturbations.

</details>


### [244] [SPO++: Stream-Aligned Policy Optimization for Asynchronous Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24870)
*Kai Ruan,Jinghao Lin,Qianshan Wei,Ziqi Zhou,Zihe Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SPO++，一种改进的单流策略优化方法。它通过修正轨迹级中心化与token级度量之间的不匹配、按策略事件而非接收顺序组织提示证据，在ALFWorld（两种模型规模）和Math-TIR上提升了在线学习效率。消融实验表明，action-token度量下的标准化是最有效的组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组相对强化学习（Group-relative RL）需要同一提示的兄弟rollout，这对于长且可变的工具使用轨迹成本高昂。先前的单流策略优化（SPO）通过持久化的提示级值估计消除了这一依赖，但其配方中每个轨迹只白化一个优势，然后优化token均值actor损失。本文指出，轨迹级中心化通常不能使actor消耗的token加权量中心化，存在度量不匹配问题。

Method: SPO++采用两种主要改进：(1) 在action-token度量下标准化终端结果优势，修正轨迹中心化与token加权量之间的不匹配；(2) 按生成提示的策略事件而非学习者接收顺序来组织提示证据。整体框架仍基于单流策略优化，使用持久化提示级值估计，无需兄弟rollout。

Result: 在ALFWorld两种模型规模以及Math-TIR上，SPO++相比SPO在在线学习效率上均有所提升。配对消融实验表明，action-token度量下的标准化是最强有效的测试组件。

Conclusion: SPO++通过修正优势标准化中的度量不匹配并改进提示证据组织方式，进一步提升了单流策略优化在长轨迹工具使用场景下的在线学习效率，验证了action-token度量标准化的重要性。

Abstract: Group-relative reinforcement learning waits for sibling rollouts of the same prompt, which is costly for long and variable tool-use trajectories. Single-stream Policy Optimization (SPO) removes this dependency with a persistent prompt-level value estimate, but its recipe whitens one advantage per trajectory before optimizing a token-mean actor loss. We show that trajectory centering generally does not center the token-weighted quantity consumed by the actor, and fix the mismatch by standardizing terminal-outcome advantages under the action-token measure. We additionally organize prompt evidence by the policy event that generated it rather than learner receipt order. Across matched runs on ALFWorld at two model scales and on Math-TIR, SPO++ improves online learning efficiency over SPO. A paired ablation identifies action-token-measure normalization as the strongest tested component.

</details>


### [245] [Recursive Experiential-Working Memory Evolution for Long-Horizon Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24876)
*Zhaochen Yu,Yingcheng Wu,Zhenfei Yin,Kaiyuan Chen,Zhe Zhao,Mengdi Wang,Shuicheng Yan,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Recuris提出一种递归经验-工作记忆架构，通过工作记忆追踪任务进度并指导技能选择，将执行过程转化为结构化证据来定位失败，并由固定元智能体进行验证门控的记忆更新，形成有界的递归记忆演化循环，在四个长程基准和十个模型上大幅提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进在长程任务中仍然困难，因为不断增长的历史记录会模糊任务状态并导致技能调用错位，现有方法难以在长交互过程中持续定位和修复失败。

Method: Recuris采用递归经验-工作记忆架构：工作记忆追踪任务进度并引导从经验记忆中选取技能，将技能使用锚定在当前需求而非完整历史；执行过程被转化为结构化证据以定位特定记忆组件中的失败；一个固定的元智能体将这些证据转化为局部化、验证门控的技能记忆更新，重塑后续执行并产生新证据，形成有界的递归记忆演化循环。

Result: 在四个长程基准和十个模型上，Recuris在37个完成的模型-基准组合中提升了35个组合的任务成功率：在tau-bench上为GPT-5.6 Sol提升+17.8分、Claude Opus 5提升+15.6分（达到87.9%），在SkillFlow上为Qwen3.6-27B/35B分别提升+16.6/+13.5分；最长任务的提升达+32.2分，常见长程失败减少高达80%。

Conclusion: 递归演化的记忆可作为RSI的可扩展基础，使智能体能够持续将积累的经验转化为日益有效的长程行为，且优势随交互时域增长而扩大。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) remains hard in long-horizon tasks, where growing histories obscure the task state and misalign skill invocation. We introduce Recuris, a recursive Experiential-Working Memory architecture for long-horizon agent harnesses, in which Working Memory tracks task progress and guides skill selection from Experiential Memory, grounding skill use in current needs rather than the full history. This coupling also turns execution into structured evidence that localizes failures to specific memory components. Across tasks, a fixed Meta-Agent turns that evidence into localized, validation-gated updates to Skill Memory that reshape execution and yield new evidence, forming a bounded recursive memory-evolution loop. Across four long-horizon benchmarks and ten models, Recuris improves task success in 35 of the 37 completed model-benchmark pairs, carrying frontier models to SOTA-level task success: on tau-bench it adds +17.8 points to GPT-5.6 Sol and +15.6 to Claude Opus 5, taking Opus 5 to 87.9%, and +16.6/+13.5 points on Qwen3.6-27B/35B on SkillFlow. The advantage widens as the interaction horizon grows, to +32.2 points on the longest tasks, and common long-horizon failures fall by up to 80%. These results position recursively evolving memory as a scalable foundation for RSI, enabling agents to continuously transform accumulated experience into increasingly effective long-horizon behavior. Code: https://github.com/Gen-Verse/Recuris

</details>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [246] [Multi-Stage Prompt-Guided Feature Modulation for Generalizable Brain Tumor Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23745)
*Mohammad Mahdi Danesh Pajouh,Sara Saeedi*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于提示条件化的nnU-Net扩展模型——多阶段动态提示nnU-Net（Multi-Stage Dynamic Prompt nnU-Net），通过在编码器最深阶段插入三个独立的动态提示模块，利用全局池化特征生成图像特定的提示表示，并通过FiLM（特征级线性调制）对特征图进行自适应条件化，以提高脑肿瘤MRI分割的鲁棒性和泛化能力。在BraTS GOAT验证集上，该模型在多数评估指标和肿瘤亚区上优于基线nnU-Net。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑肿瘤MRI分割对诊断、治疗规划和手术导航至关重要，但现有自动分割模型在不同肿瘤特征、成像协议、采集中心和患者群体间的泛化能力不足，肿瘤形态和成像分布的变化会导致域外性能显著下降，因此提升深度学习分割模型的鲁棒性和泛化性成为医学图像分析的关键目标。

Method: 提出Multi-Stage Dynamic Prompt nnU-Net，是nnU-Net的提示条件化扩展。在编码器最深的三个阶段分别插入三个独立的动态提示模块，每个模块包含可学习的10个256维提示向量库，利用全局池化编码特征生成图像特定提示表示，并将这些表示投影为特征级缩放(γ)和偏移(β)参数，通过FiLM（特征级线性调制）对编码器特征图进行多语义层次的自适应条件化调制。

Result: 在BraTS GOAT验证集上，所提模型在多数评估指标和肿瘤亚区上优于基线nnU-Net。病变级平均Dice分数达到76.16%（ET）、80.04%（TC）和86.42%（WT），而基线模型分别为74.38%、78.14%和84.01%。

Conclusion: 多阶段动态提示条件化能够有效提升脑肿瘤分割的准确性和边界描绘质量，验证了该方法的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Accurate brain tumor segmentation from magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is essential for diagnosis, treatment planning, surgical guidance, and disease monitoring. However, developing automated segmentation models that generalize across diverse tumor characteristics, imaging protocols, acquisition sites, and patient populations remains challenging. Variations in tumor morphology and imaging distributions can substantially degrade performance outside the training domain. Consequently, improving the robustness and generalization of deep learning-based segmentation models has become a key objective in medical image analysis. To improve segmentation robustness, we propose Multi-Stage Dynamic Prompt nnU-Net, a prompt-conditioned extension of nnU-Net. Three independent dynamic prompt modules are inserted into the deepest encoder stages. Each module contains a learnable bank of ten 256-dimensional prompt vectors and uses globally pooled encoder features to generate image-specific prompt representations. These representations are projected into feature-wise scaling $(γ)$ and shifting $(β)$ parameters that modulate encoder feature maps through Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), enabling adaptive feature conditioning at multiple semantic levels. Evaluation on the BraTS GOAT validation dataset demonstrated that the proposed Multi-Stage Dynamic Prompt nnU-Net outperformed the baseline nnU Net across the majority of evaluated metrics and tumor subregions. The proposed model achieved average lesion-wise Dice scores of 76.16% (ET), 80.04% (TC), and 86.42% (WT), compared with 74.38%, 78.14% and 84.01% for the baseline model. The results demonstrate that multi-stage dynamic prompt conditioning improves segmentation accuracy and boundary delineation for brain tumor segmentation.

</details>


### [247] [Spatiotemporal Distillation via Recurrent Bottlenecks for Aortic Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23879)
*Dexter Wen Jie Teo,Nairouz Shehata,Herve Lombaert*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于心脏电影MRI主动脉分割的半监督时空(2D→2D+t)知识蒸馏框架，通过动态潜空间截取和循环时空瓶颈增强跨帧一致性，在仅有限标注下显著提升分割的时空调制性和结构可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准2D分割网络逐帧独立处理，在快速收缩期血流遮挡主动脉边界时缺乏连续上下文，导致帧间跟踪丢失和边界不一致；而时空(2D+t)网络虽能强制时序一致性但缺乏专家标注。

Method: 提出半监督时空知识蒸馏框架，将空间教师模型的知识蒸馏到时空学生网络。通过动态潜空间截取(dynamic latent interception)，配对循环时空瓶颈(recurrent spatiotemporal bottleneck)与残差空间旁路(residual spatial bypass)。模型选择策略先应用基线验证阈值(DSC≥0.50)，再选择最大化解剖一致性(anatomical consistency)的epoch。

Result: 时空学生模型实现表面跟踪精度NSD@1mm=92.3%±0.2%，结构可靠性Frac_2CC=99.2%±0.6%，相比2D nnU-Net基线将群体结构异常降低超过56%。

Conclusion: 该半监督知识蒸馏框架能有效利用心脏周期信息，在标注稀缺条件下同时兼顾时空一致性与解剖结构可靠性，为主动脉硬度等心血管指标测量提供稳健的分割工具。

Abstract: Cardiac cine-MRI serves as a direct visual indicator of cardiovascular hemodynamics by capturing the continuous wall motion of the aorta. Quantifying these dynamic structural changes across the cardiac cycle is essential for measuring aortic distensibility, a primary marker of arterial stiffness. However, standard 2D segmentation networks focus on each frame independently. Consequently, when rapid systolic flow temporarily obscures the aorta's boundaries, this lack of continuous context results in frame-to-frame tracking dropouts and boundary inconsistencies. Spatiotemporal ($2\text{D}+t$) networks can enforce temporal consistency across the sequence but suffer from a scarcity of expert annotations. To address this, we present a semi-supervised spatiotemporal ($2\text{D}$ to $2\text{D}+t$) knowledge distillation framework exploiting the cardiac cycle. The framework distills a spatial teacher's expertise into a spatiotemporal student network by executing a dynamic latent interception, pairing a recurrent spatiotemporal bottleneck with a residual spatial bypass. Our model selection strategy applies a baseline validation threshold ($\text{DSC} \ge 0.50$) prior to selecting the epoch that maximizes anatomical consistency. This strategy enables the spatiotemporal student model to achieve superior surface tracking accuracy ($\text{NSD@1mm} = 92.3\% \pm 0.2\%$) and high structural reliability ($\text{Frac}_{2\text{CC}} = 99.2\% \pm 0.6\%$), reducing population-wide structural anomalies by over 56\% compared to a 2D nnU-Net baseline.

</details>


### [248] [Native-Space 3D CarveMix for Multi-Site T1w Stroke Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23882)
*Dexter Wen Jie Teo,Kumaradevan Punithakumar*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合MedNeXt-L骨干网络与3D CarveMix数据增强的方法，用于跨中心、无强度标准化的T1加权MRI缺血性卒中病灶分割。在ISLES 2026挑战的1,453例扫描数据上，该方法达到0.648的Dice分数，较基线提升0.018。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨不同扫描仪和协议采集的T1w MRI上分割缺血性卒中病灶极具挑战，因为病灶细微且与脑脊液的强度特征相似。现有标准深度学习架构在多中心训练中Dice仅约0.66即陷入平台期，其中急性病灶（卒中后≤7天）由于严重样本稀缺而表现更差。

Method: 采用MedNeXt-L（k=5）骨干网络，结合训练时动态进行的3D CarveMix增强，即实时将真实病灶斑块粘贴到健康脑区。通过在每个折内以主体级隔离进行合成病灶位置的动态生成，模型无需在磁盘上预生成副本即可看到更多样化的病灶模式。

Result: 在来自55个临床中心的1,453例原生T1w扫描上进行评估，500轮训练后达到平均5折交叉验证Dice为0.648，较相同训练预算下的MedNeXt-L骨干网络基线（0.630）提升0.018。

Conclusion: CarveMix增强能够有效缓解缺血性卒中病灶分割中的数据稀缺问题，尤其在无强度标准化和跨中心场景下，以轻量计算开销带来稳定的分割性能提升。

Abstract: Segmenting ischemic stroke lesions on T1-weighted (T1w) MRI acquired across different scanners and protocols without intensity standardization is difficult because lesions are subtle and share intensity characteristics with cerebrospinal fluid. Standard deep learning architectures trained across multiple centers plateau around Dice 0.66, with acute lesions ($\le 7$ days post-stroke) performing substantially worse due to severe sample scarcity. We combine a MedNeXt-L ($k=5$) backbone with on-the-fly 3D CarveMix augmentation that pastes real lesion patches into healthy brain regions during training. By generating synthetic lesion placements dynamically within each fold with subject-level split isolation, the model sees more diverse lesion patterns without requiring pre-generated copies on disk. We evaluate on 1,453 native T1w scans from 55 clinical centers in the ISLES 2026 challenge. Our method achieves a mean 5-fold cross-validation Dice of 0.648 at 500 epochs, a +0.018 improvement over the MedNeXt-L backbone at a matched training budget (0.630)

</details>


### [249] [Model Effect or Label Effect? Refined Annotations and a Human-Referenced Benchmark for Pulmonary Embolism Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24486)
*Qihang Sun,Zhongxiao Liu,Bailiang Jian,Shenman Qiu,Jingyuan Wang,Lei Zhang,Lixiang Xie,Jiazhen Pan,Christian Wachinger*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本研究量化了评价注释（evaluation annotations）对肺栓塞（PE）分割性能指标的影响，发现仅更换注释可使DSC提升0.143-0.188，远超改变训练数据集构成的影响（0.028）。作者建立了基于人类标注者参照的评估框架（nnPE），但nnPE得分低于所有四位标注者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺栓塞分割模型的性能评估高度依赖评价注释的质量，但注释差异对测量结果的影响程度鲜有量化。本研究旨在系统分离"注释效应"（label effect）与"模型效应"（model effect），明确二者对性能指标贡献的相对大小，并建立人类参照的评估框架。

Method: 回顾性纳入166例体素标注的CT肺动脉造影病例（来自CADPE、FUMPE、READ三个数据集，149例入组）。一级标注者按协议标注PE，高级胸部放射科医生审查修正所有分割；另有3位标注者在三个中心标注15例子集。通过对比两个预训练nnU-Net模型在原始与精炼注释下的表现衡量标注效应；通过固定注释、改变训练数据集组合衡量模型效应。基准模型nnPE采用leave-one-dataset-out和五折交叉验证训练。采用病例配对Wilcoxon符号秩检验、Benjamini-Hochberg校正及bootstrap 95% CI分析四个指标类别。

Result: 仅更换注释使nnU-Net-A的DSC提高0.143（0.122-0.166），nnU-Net-B提高0.188（0.163-0.213）（均P<.001），而改变训练数据集构成仅使DSC变化0.028。标注效应在CADPE和FUMPE上超过模型效应，在READ上为0.045。重新标注后三个数据集的掩膜内衰减标准差均显著下降（均P<.001）。nnPE在合并交叉验证中达到DSC 0.72±0.22，但在52次配对比较中均低于四位标注者（所有校正后P<.05）。

Conclusion: 评价注释对PE分割性能测量结果的影响至少与模型训练选择相当甚至更大。研究公开提供了人类参照的评估框架，供未来研究使用。

Abstract: Purpose: To quantify how evaluation annotations influence measured pulmonary embolism (PE) segmentation performance relative to model training changes, and to establish a human-referenced framework.
  Materials and Methods: This retrospective study screened 166 voxel-annotated CT pulmonary angiography cases from CADPE (n=91), FUMPE (n=35), and READ (n=40); 149 were included. A primary rater annotated PE by protocol, and a senior thoracic radiologist reviewed and revised all segmentations. Three additional raters at three centers annotated a 15-case subset. The label effect was measured by evaluating two pretrained nnU-Net models (nnU-Net-A, nnU-Net-B) against original and refined annotations. The model effect was measured by comparing the same architecture trained on different dataset combinations with annotations fixed. The benchmark model (nnPE) was trained with leave-one-dataset-out and pooled five-fold cross-validation. Four metric categories were analyzed with case-paired Wilcoxon signed-rank tests, Benjamini-Hochberg correction, and bootstrap 95% CIs.
  Results: Changing only the annotation increased mean DSC by 0.143 (0.122-0.166) for nnU-Net-A and 0.188 (0.163-0.213) for nnU-Net-B (both P < .001), whereas changing training-dataset composition changed DSC by 0.028. The label effect exceeded the model effect on CADPE and FUMPE and was 0.045 on READ. Within-mask attenuation SD fell in all three datasets after re-annotation (all P < .001). nnPE reached DSC 0.72 +/- 0.22 on pooled cross-validation but scored below all four annotators across 52 paired comparisons (all corrected P < .05).
  Conclusion: Evaluation annotations affected measured PE segmentation performance at least as much as model training choices. A human-referenced evaluation framework is publicly available for future study.

</details>


### [250] [Score-Based Ideal Observer Approximation via Denoising Score Matching for Signal-Known-Exactly Detection Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24768)
*Weimin Zhou*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于得分函数的贝叶斯理想观察者（SIO），利用仅在信号缺失图像上训练的降噪卷积神经网络估计得分函数，从而近似理想观察者检测统计量，无需逐图像后验采样或针对特定信号重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯理想观察者（IO）是二元检测任务性能的理论上界，但其检验统计量的解析计算通常不可行。现有的MCMC方法需要大量后验采样，监督学习方法则需要针对特定任务和信号进行重训练，缺乏通用性。

Method: 将IO检验统计量用得分函数（对数概率密度的梯度）重新表述，提出SIO方法。使用仅在信号缺失图像上训练的降噪卷积神经网络估计信号缺失分布得分函数，该模型训练完成后可直接用于任意加性信号的检测任务，无需逐图像后验采样或信号特定重训练。

Result: 在具有随机集块（lumpy）背景模型的信号已知精确（SKE）检测任务中，数值实验表明所提出的SIO能够紧密逼近真实IO性能。

Conclusion: SIO通过得分函数重表述将IO检验统计量转化为可学习的形式，训练一次即可适用于任意加性信号的检测任务，兼具计算效率与IO近似精度，为贝叶斯理想观察者的实用化提供了新途径。

Abstract: The Bayesian Ideal Observer (IO) establishes the theoretical upper bound on task performance for binary detection tasks. However, analytical computation of the IO test statistic is generally intractable. Numerical approaches based on Markov-chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) methods, including their recent deep generative model-based extensions, typically require extensive posterior sampling for each test image. Supervised learning has also been investigated to approximate the IO performance. However, such methods are typically trained for a specific detection task and signal and may require retraining when the task or signal changes. The score function, defined as the gradient of the log probability density, encodes the local geometry of the data distribution and is a fundamental quantity in modern score-based generative modeling. This work reformulates the IO test statistic in terms of the score function and introduces a score-based ideal observer (SIO). The proposed SIO uses a denoising convolutional neural network trained exclusively on signal-absent images to estimate the signal-absent score function. Once trained, the resulting score model can be used to approximate the IO test statistic for detection tasks involving arbitrary additive signals, without per-image posterior sampling or signal-specific retraining. Numerical studies consider a signal-known-exactly (SKE) detection task with a stochastic lumpy-background model. The results demonstrate that the proposed SIO can closely approximate the IO performance.

</details>
